```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(30):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
  if diff>0:
   mask=h<1-1e-15
   room=(1-h)[mask].sum()
   if room==0: break
   h[mask]+=diff/room
  else:
   mask=h>1e-15
   tot=h[mask].sum()
   if tot==0: break
   h[mask]+=diff/tot  # negative
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return np.where(np.abs(h-1)<1e-15,1,h)
```
This distributes to all non-bound proportionally, can converge fast. Or simpler add diff/n and clip loop.

But exact equality for sum: Floating. We can at end adjust one non-bound variable by residual. But if residual tiny, okay. `np.sum(best_h)*dx` should be 1 maybe within 1e-12. To be safe, final exact adjust:
```
def exact_sum_normalize(h,n):

 # choose index with largest min(h,1-h) and adjust? If residual <= that room.

 # We can add small to many non-saturated to keep in bounds.
 for _ in range(10):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
  if diff>0:
   idx=np.argmax(1-h)
   step=min(1-h[idx], diff)
  else:
   idx=np.argmin(h)
   step=max(h[idx], diff) # negative
  h[idx]+=step
  if abs(target-np.sum(h))>1e-12 and abs(step)>0:
     # distribute remaining? maybe
```

def make_active_weights(c, n, delta_counts, min_active=8):
    L=len(c)
    cmax=c.max()
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    k = min(L, max(min_active, int(0.005*L)))
    dk = c[order[k-1]]
    level = max(cmax - delta_counts, dk - 0.5)
    x = np.zeros(L)
    mask = c >= level
    x[mask]=c[mask]-level + 1e-12
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-15:
       x[order[:k]]=1
       x/=k
    else: x/=s
    return x
```
But if k=8 and top all equal, level = dk-0.5, x small? okay.
For cmax maybe large, x values up to delta. Weighted objective approximates average over active lags above threshold, not exactly max but good.

Let's derive `weighted_gradient` using conv formula with B=w.
```
def weighted_gradient(h, w, n):
    N=n-1
    a1h = 1.0 - h
    # conv length n+L-1 = 3n-2? n=2 length4, yes.
    # Use np.convolve(a1h, w[::-1], mode='full')[N:N+n]
    # and np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[N:N+n]
    g1 = np.convolve(a1h, w[::-1], mode='full')
    g2 = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')
    return g1[N:N+n] - g2[N:N+n]
```
Need confirm slice length: np.convolve length 3n-2. N:N+n = n elements. For n=1 N=0, conv len1, slice0:1 okay. For n=2 len4, slice1:3? Wait N=1 to N+n =1:3 (indices 1,2) length2. Earlier we needed index p+N: p=0 ->1, p=1 ->2. Yes slice [1:3]. Good.
But earlier for G1 n=2 we used C1, C2? Conv C length4 indices 0,1,2,3. We need p=0->1, p=1->2. G1 p=1 expected w0(1-a?) no invalid? direct: p=1 q=-1: A0 w0; q=0:A1 w1; q=1 invalid => (1-a)w0+(1-b)w1. Conv C2 = A0*w0? Let's compute B_rev=[w2,w1,w0]. C2 = A0*B_rev2 + A1*B_rev1 = (1-a)w0+(1-b)w1 yes.
G2 slice conv(h,w) index p+N: p=0 index1 = H0w1 + H1w0, matches; p=1 index2 = H1w1? conv C2=H0w2+H1w1? Wait conv length4: H*B: C0=H0w0, C1=H0w1+H1w0, C2=H0w2+H1w1. For p=1, D expected q=-1 H2 invalid? p=1: q=-1 -> H2? invalid, q=0 H1w1, q=1 H0w2. So D=H1w1+H0w2. index2 yes. Good.

def wcd_polish(h, n, deadline, reserve=30):
    S = n/2
    dx = 2/n
    h = project_feasible(h,n)

           pass_count +=1

              A = mu - w[N]

Option 1: soft hinge:
```
def make_weights(c, n, delta):
    L=len(c)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    level = cmax - delta
    x = np.maximum(c - level, 0.0)
    s = x.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       x[:]=0.0; m=int(min(L, max(10, L//100))); order=np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]; x[order]=1; s=m
    # power to emphasize near max:
    x = x*x? if x>0
    w = x / (x.sum() + L*1e-8)
    # add tiny floor?
    w += 1e-8
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```

```
def make_weights(c, n, delta, power=2, min_fract=0.01):
    L=len(c); cmax=float(c.max())
    level=cmax-delta
    x=np.maximum(c-level,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    k=max(8, int(0.008*L))
    if s < 1e-10:
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        x=np.zeros(L); x[idx]=1.0; s=k
    else:
        # ensure at least k lags nonzero? If fewer than k active, lower level to c[k-1]
        active_count = int((x>0).sum())
        if active_count < k:
            # find kth largest
            kth = np.partition(c, L-k)[L-k] maybe 0-index? 
            level = max(level, kth - 0.5) # ensure kth included
            x = np.maximum(c-level,0.0)
            s=x.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: ...
    if power != 1: x = np.power(x, power) where >0
    s=x.sum()
    w=x/s
    w += 1e-7 # floor maybe
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```

import numpy as np
import time

def c5_count(h,n): return max(correlate)
def eval_c5(h,n): dx=2.0/n; return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))*dx)

`project_feasible(h,n)`:
Need efficient and exact. Let's implement uniform distribution:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(50):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-13
            if not np.any(mask): break
            vals = h[mask]
            room = (1.0 - vals).sum()
            if room <= 1e-15: break
            h[mask] = np.clip(vals + diff/room, 0.0,1.0)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-13
            if not np.any(mask): break
            vals=h[mask]
            tot=vals.sum()
            if tot <=1e-15: break
            h[mask] = np.clip(vals + diff/tot, 0.0,1.0)
    # final small correction distributed over interior all
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
         mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
         if np.any(mask): h[mask]+=diff/n # maybe enough
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But last diff/n may violate if many near bounds; but diff small. Could call one more loop after. Simpler keep loop.
At end, to make sum exactly within float, we can adjust all non-bound by `diff/len(mask)`; but might exceed. Use iterative for 5 more. Good.

Maybe use scaling for negative diff: `vals + diff/tot` multiplies by (1 + diff/tot), works. For positive, add proportional to room; not exactly preserve shape but fine.

`make_weights(c, n, slack_count, min_active=12)`:
```
def make_weights(c, n, slack_count, min_active=12):
    L=len(c); cmax=float(c.max())
    level = cmax - slack_count
    if level < 0: level = -slack_count*0.1
    x = np.maximum(c - level, 0.0)
    act = x > 1e-14
    if int(act.sum()) < min_active:
        k = min(L, min_active)
        # kth largest
        thr = np.partition(c, L-k)[L-k]
        level = max(level, thr - 0.25)
        x = np.maximum(c - level, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-14:
        k = min(L, min_active)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, L-k)[L-k:] if k<L else np.arange(L)
        x = np.zeros(L); x[idx]=1.0; s=float(k)
    # power 2 to emphasize near-max
    # But if many active, okay.
    x2 = np.zeros(L)
    act = x > 1e-14
    x2[act] = x[act]*x[act]
    s2 = float(x2.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-14:
       w = x/s
    else:
       w = x2/s2
    # add small floor over all lags? Maybe 1e-6
    floor = 1e-7
    w = w + floor
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
If cmax around 100, slack maybe 0.5, x small. power2 okay. If one active dominates, floor prevents zero but still near.
Need ensure min_active includes central lag if slack too small? If central not in top min_active? It is initially max often. Later max maybe other. If central not active but high, scaling. okay.

`weighted_gradient(h,w,n)`:
Use np.convolve. Need speed. For real arrays, okay. But `np.convolve` may be O(N^2) and for n=1500 maybe 4.5M per conv, two 9M, per weight update maybe okay. Could use FFT for larger n to be safe. We can implement adaptive:
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w,n):
    N=n-1
    # if n<600 direct, else fft? Actually np.convolve direct length 1000x1999=2M multiplications; FFT overhead maybe. Use direct for simplicity.
    g1 = np.convolve(1.0-h, w[::-1], mode='full')[N:N+n]
    g2 = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[N:N+n]
    return g1-g2
```
Potential memory: returns length 3n-2 ~ 4498 for n=1500, fine. It copies full. Could use `mode='full'` but only slice; np.convolve computes full. Could use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve` for n>800. Let's consider importing `from scipy.signal import fftconvolve`. It may be faster for n>1000. Use try:
```
try: from scipy.signal import fftconvolve as _fftconvolve
except: _fftconvolve=None
```
In function, if _fftconvolve is not None and n>500: `g1 = _fftconvolve(1-h, w[::-1], mode='full')[N:N+n]`. But for small arrays, fft overhead maybe. We can set threshold 512. This improves large n. Need no closures. Top-level import okay.

`wcd_optimize(h, n, deadline, reserve, rng, label, max_stages?)`:
We need return best_h, best_c, final_h maybe. It will modify local h. Use time checks.
Parameters: `time_limit_s` maybe max duration for this optimization, or use deadline absolute.
Let's design:
```
def wcd_optimize(h, n, deadline, min_stages=6, stage_max_passes=400, reserve=15):
    t0=time.time()
    S=0.5*n
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    slacks_C = [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0004,0.0001,0.00004]
    # maybe min stages by time: if deadline-t0 < 60, use 3 stages
    num_stages = len(slacks_C)
    for stage in range(num_stages):
        if time.time() > deadline - reserve: break
        c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
        cmax=float(c.max())
        slack_count = max(0.05, (n/2.0)*slacks_C[stage])
        # If slack_count > cmax*0.4? cap? For initial cmax maybe 175? okay.
        w = make_weights(c,n,slack_count)
        G=weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
        pair = w + w[::-1]
        wN=float(w[n-1])
        mu = max(1.5, wN + 0.5)
        # If wN close mu and A small, increase mu
        Acoef = mu - wN
        if Acoef < 0.25:
            mu = wN + 0.5
            Acoef=0.5
        diff=float(h.sum()-S)
        # pass loop
        maxd=1.0
        pass_count=0
        pass_cap = 400
        # time per stage? Use if stage time > 15%? We can allow loop while time < deadline-reserve and (pass_count<pass_cap and maxd>1e-10)
        # But a single stage could consume all if not converging. To ensure multiple stages, allocate stage_time_limit = max(10, (deadline-t0-reserve)/num_stages * 1.5?)
        stage_deadline = min(deadline-reserve, time.time() + max(10.0, (deadline-time.time()-reserve)*0.18))
        while time.time() < stage_deadline and pass_count < pass_cap and maxd > 1e-11:
            pass_count += 1
            maxd = 0.0
            # order: natural or random? Use natural for reproducibility; maybe reverse alternate to avoid bias.
            for p in range(n):
                h0 = h[p]
                B = G[p] + 2.0*mu*diff
                d = -B / (2.0*Acoef)
                lo = -h0
                hi = 1.0 - h0
                if d < lo: d = lo
                elif d > hi: d = hi
                # optional damp if |d| > 0.5 and cmax? We can damp first half passes?
                if abs(d) > 0.5:
                    # keep but clip? full bound <=1. 
                    pass
                if abs(d) > 1e-12:
                    h[p] = h0 + d
                    diff += d
                    if abs(d) > maxd: maxd = abs(d)
                    qlo = p - (n-1)
                    if qlo < 0: qlo = 0
                    qhi = p + (n-1)
                    if qhi >= n: qhi = n-1
                    klo = (n-1) + (qlo - p)
                    G[qlo:qhi+1] -= pair[klo:klo+(qhi-qlo+1)] * d
            # if sum drift large, project and recompute G? 
            if abs(diff) > 0.25:
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                G = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
                diff = float(h.sum()-S)
                maxd = 0.1
        # end stage
        h = project_feasible(h,n)
        c5=eval_c5(h,n)
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c=c5; best_h=h.copy()
        # If current much worse than best, maybe continue from best? We'll decide.
        if c5 > best_c + 1e-6:
            # if only slightly worse, continue; if much worse, restore best to avoid bad basin
            # maybe if c5 > best_c + 1e-4: h=best_h.copy()
            if c5 > best_c + 1e-4:
                h = best_h.copy()
                # recompute next stage from best
    return best_h, best_c, h
```

 return best.

def feasible_for_L(c,a,h0,L,dlo,dhi,n,diag_c,diag_a):
    lo=dlo; hi=dhi
    # zero mask: need min zero c <=L; but maybe avoid each iteration by precomputing max_zero? If c[zero]>L infeas. Since L increases, if max_zero>L. Precompute c_zero_max.
    if c_zero_max > L: return None
    if pos: xhi_candidates = (L - c[pos]) / a[pos]; hi=min(hi, xhi_candidates.min())
    if neg: xlo_candidates = (L - c[neg]) / a[neg]; lo=max(lo, xlo_candidates.max())
    if lo > hi + 1e-12: return None
    # diag
    if has_diag:
       B=diag_a; C=L-diag_c; disc=B*B-4*C
       if disc >=0:
          r1=(B-sqrt(disc))/2; r2=(B+sqrt(disc))/2
          # Feasible if [lo,hi] not entirely inside (r1,r2)
          if not (hi < r1 + tol or lo > r2 - tol):
              # Could still have intervals [lo,min(hi,r1)] or [max(lo,r2),hi]
              if hi < r1 + tol:
                  pass
              elif lo > r2 - tol:
                  pass
              else:
                  # if [lo,hi] intersects outside; feasible if lo <= r1 or hi >= r2
                  if lo > r1 + tol and hi < r2 - tol:
                      return None
                  # keep but need candidate intervals
    return (lo,hi)

def reverse_slice(arr,start,length):
    if length <=0: return empty
    stop = start - length
    if stop >= 0:
       return arr[start:stop:-1]
    else:
       return arr[start::-1][:length]
```

Now `feasible_for_L` vector functions. To avoid passing many arrays, create a class? no closures. Use top-level function with arguments.
```
def exact_feasible(c_zero_max, cpos,apos,cneg,aneg, diag_c, diag_a, L, dlo, dhi):
    lo=dlo; hi=dhi
    if c_zero_max > L + 1e-13: return None
    if apos.size:
       val = np.min((L - cpos)/apos)
       if val < hi: hi = val
    if aneg.size:
       val = np.max((L - cneg)/aneg)
       if val > lo: lo = val
    if lo > hi + 1e-12: return None
    roots=None
    # diag
    B=diag_a; C=L-diag_c
    disc=B*B - 4*C
    if disc >= 0:
       sqrt=np.sqrt(max(0,disc))
       r1=0.5*(B-sqrt); r2=0.5*(B+sqrt)
       roots=(r1,r2)
       if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
           return None
    # also ensure lo/hi finite
    if not np.isfinite(lo) or not np.isfinite(hi): return None
    lo = max(lo,dlo); hi=min(hi,dhi)
    if lo > hi + 1e-12: return None
    return (lo,hi,roots)
```
But `c_zero_max` for zero a positions: if any zero coeff and base c > L infeas. If no zero positions, set -inf.
`apos`, `aneg` can be empty; `np.min` on empty error.

Potential use of `np.argpartition` in make_weights with k; if k==L, handle. L up to 1999, fine.

`make_weights` detailed code:
```
def make_weights(c, n, slack_count, min_active=12):
    L = len(c)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    level = cmax - slack_count
    x = np.maximum(c - level, 0.0)
    act = int((x > 0.0).sum())
    k = max(2, min(L, min_active))
    if act < k:
        if k < L:
            thr = np.partition(c, L-k)[L-k]
        else:
            thr = c.min()
        level2 = max(level, thr - 0.25)
        x = np.maximum(c - level2, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-14:
        if k < L:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L-k)[L-k:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        x = np.zeros(L)
        x[idx] = 1.0
        s = float(x[idx].size)
    p2 = x*x
    s2 = float(p2.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-14:
        w = x/s
    else:
        w = p2/s2
    w += 1e-8
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
Power square over weights may make sum weights still 1. If x max 10, p2 max 100, top dominates. Good.
Floor 1e-8 negligible.

STAGE_SLACKS = (0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0004, 0.00012, 0.00004)
```

In `wcd_optimize`, choose pass_cap based on n:
```

elif n <= 1200: pass_cap=220
else: pass_cap=140
```
Maybe too many. Use 300/200/120.
Stage loop:
```
num_stages = len(STAGE_SLACKS)
for stage in range(num_stages):
    if time.time() > deadline: break
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    c5 = float(np.max(c))*2/n
    if c5 < best_c: ...
    slack_count = max(0.02, 0.5*n*STAGE_SLACKS[stage])
    w = make_weights(c,n,slack_count,16)
    G = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
    pair = w + w[::-1]
    wN = float(w[n-1])
    mu = 1.0 + max(wN, 0.5)
    Acoef = mu - wN
    diff = float(h.sum() - 0.5*n)
    maxd=1.0
    passes=0
    while passes < pass_cap and maxd > 1e-9 and time.time() < deadline:
        passes +=1
        maxd=0.0
        for p in range(n):
            h0 = h[p]
            B = G[p] + 2.0*mu*diff
            d = -B / (2.0*Acoef)
            lo = -h0; hi = 1.0-h0
            if d < lo: d=lo
            elif d > hi: d=hi
            if abs(d) > 1e-12:
                h[p] = h0 + d
                diff += d
                if abs(d)>maxd: maxd=abs(d)
                N=n-1
                qlo = p-N; if qlo<0: qlo=0
                qhi = p+N; if qhi>=n: qhi=n-1
                klo = N + (qlo - p)
                length = qhi - qlo + 1
                G[qlo:qhi+1] -= pair[klo:klo+length] * d
        if abs(diff) > 0.5:
            h = project_feasible(h,n)
            G = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
            diff = float(h.sum() - 0.5*n)
            maxd=0.1
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c5 = eval_c5(h,n)
    if c5 < best_c: best...
    if c5 > best_c + 1e-3:
       # If current is too far, revert to best for subsequent stages
       h = best_h.copy()
```

def make_start(ref_h, n, kind, param, rng):
    if kind == 0:
       if len(ref_h)==n: h=ref_h.copy()
       else: xold=np.linspace(0,2,len(ref_h)); xnew=np.linspace(0,2,n); h=np.interp(xnew,xold,ref_h)
    elif kind == 1:
       base = interpolate if needed
       sigma=param
       h=base + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
    else:
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       f=param
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h=0.5 + 0.48*np.sin(np.pi*f*x + phase)
       # maybe add second harmonic to break symmetry:
       h += 0.15*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def exact_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval; c=correlate; dx=2/n; current_max=c.max()*dx
    diff=0; D=0.1
    max_steps = 600? no
    pass_count=0
    while time < deadline and pass_count < 8:
       pass_count +=1
       max_move=0.0
       order = rng.permutation(n) if pass_count>1 else range(n) # natural first? Random later.
       for p in order:
           if time > deadline: break
           h0=h[p]
           # if h0 too close bound? still maybe move.
           dlo = max(-h0, -D - diff, -0.1)
           dhi = min(1-h0, D - diff, 0.1)
           if dlo >= dhi: continue? if interval has no positive/negative? if dlo==dhi no move.
           a = coord_a(h,p,n)
           d = propose_d(c,a,h0,dlo,dhi,n)
           # limit by step 0.1 already
           if abs(d) > 1e-10:
               # evaluate by updating copy? We can apply and compute new c; if worse revert.
               c += a*d
               c[n-1] -= d*d
               new_max = float(c.max())*dx
               if new_max <= current_max + 1e-10:
                   h[p]=h0+d; diff += d; current_max = min(current_max, new_max) # use new_max
                   if abs(d)>max_move: max_move=abs(d)
                   # update local best? Need projected; can't because sum drift. We can still store unprojected? At end project. For now maybe if diff small? We'll update after projection.
               else:
                   c -= a*d
                   c[n-1] += d*d
       # after pass
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
           h = project_feasible(h,n)
           diff = 0.0
       c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       current_max = float(c.max())*dx
       if current_max < best_c:
          best_c=current_max; best_h=h.copy()
       if max_move < 1e-8: break
       if time > deadline: break
    # final project and eval best
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```

`coord_a` top-level:
```
def coord_a(h,p,n):
    N=n-1; L=2*n-1
    a=np.zeros(L)
    m1=N-p
    if m1<0: m1=0
    m2=2*N-p
    if m2>L-1: m2=L-1
    if m1<=m2:
       ln=m2-m1+1
       jf=p+m1-N
       a[m1:m2+1] += 1.0 - h[jf:jf+ln]
    m1b=p
    if m1b<0: m1b=0
    m2b=p+N
    if m2b>L-1: m2b=L-1
    if m1b<=m2b:
       ln=m2b-m1b+1
       jb=p-m1b+N
       a[m1b:m2b+1] -= reverse_slice(h,jb,ln)
    return a
```
Check forward m2=2N-p: q=m-N, p+q=N? if m=2N-p, q=N-p, p+q=N, valid. Good.
Backward m2b=p+N: q=p, jb=p-q=0; valid. Good.

`reverse_slice` top-level:
```
def reverse_slice(arr, start, length):
    if length <=0:
       return np.empty(0)
    stop = start - length
    if stop >= 0:
       return arr[start:stop:-1]
    return arr[start::-1][:length]
```
If stop=0, else includes. If stop<0, else. Good.

`exact_feasible_interval` arguments many. To reduce, maybe combine into one function but Python overhead okay. Need no closures.
```
def exact_feasible(czero, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, diag_c, diag_a, L, dlo, dhi):
   lo=dlo; hi=dhi
   if czero > L + 1e-12: return None
   if apos.size:
       v=float(np.min((L-cpos)/apos))
       if v < hi: hi=v
   if aneg.size:
       v=float(np.max((L-cneg)/aneg))
       if v > lo: lo=v
   if lo > hi + 1e-11: return None
   B=diag_a; C=L-diag_c
   disc=B*B - 4.0*C
   roots=None
   if disc >= 0.0:
       r1=(B-math.sqrt(max(0,disc)))*0.5
       r2=(B+math.sqrt(max(0,disc)))*0.5
       roots=(r1,r2)
       if lo > r1 + 1e-11 and hi < r2 - 1e-11:
           return None
   if lo < dlo: lo=dlo
   if hi > dhi: hi=dhi
   if lo > hi + 1e-11: return None
   return (lo,hi,roots)
```
Use math.sqrt import math. Good.

`propose_d`:
```
def propose_d(c,a,h0,dlo,dhi,n):
    if dlo > dhi: return 0.0
    N=n-1
    eps=1e-13
    pos=a > eps
    neg=a < -eps
    cpos=c[pos]; apos=a[pos]
    cneg=c[neg]; aneg=a[neg]
    zero=~(pos|neg)
    czero=float(c[zero].max()) if np.any(zero) else -1e30
    diag_c=float(c[N]); diag_a=float(a[N])
    cmax=float(c.max())
    loL=0.0; hiL=cmax
    best_res=None
    # If d=0 not in interval? ensure.
    for _ in range(26):
       L=0.5*(loL+hiL)
       res=exact_feasible(czero,cpos,apos,cneg,aneg,diag_c,diag_a,L,dlo,dhi)
       if res is None:
          loL=L
       else:
          hiL=L; best_res=res
    # candidates list as Python list to avoid np.concatenate many
    cands=[dlo,dhi,0.0]
    if best_res is not None:
        lo,hi,roots=best_res
        cands.extend([lo,hi,0.5*(lo+hi)])
        if roots is not None:
            cands.extend(roots)
    best_d=0.0
    best_val=cmax
    for d in cands:
        if d < dlo - 1e-12 or d > dhi + 1e-12: continue
        d=float(np.clip(d,dlo,dhi))
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            val=cmax
        else:
            temp = c + a*d
            temp[N] -= d*d
            val=float(temp.max())
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val=val; best_d=d
    # If best_d 0 but linear interval suggests? okay.
    return best_d
```
But bisection with loL=0: If no feasible L until maybe >cmax? d=0 feasible so hiL should feasible. At L=cmax, feasible interval includes d=0 unless d=0 outside? We ensure dlo<=0<=dhi. So best_res not None. If due to diagonal roots exclude 0? At L=cmax, 0 should satisfy diag ≤cmax (new diag at d=0 = cmax if cmax maybe not diag; diag could be ≤cmax). Yes.

Potential issue: `czero` computed from positions where a=0. But if zero position has c greater than L, infeas. Good. If no zero positions, set -1.
`apos.size` is int; use `apos.size > 0`.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-3.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       base_h=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       n_base=int(len(base_h))
    except Exception:
       n_base=500
       base_h=np.full(n_base,0.5)
    if n_base < 50:
       n_base=50; base_h=np.linspace(0,1,n_base)?
    best_h=project_feasible(base_h,n_base)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n_base)
    # exact reserve depending n_best
    def exact_reserve_for(n): return 200 if n<=800 else 250
    # Since no closures, top-level helper `est_exact_reserve(n)`
    reserve=est_res(n_base)
    main_deadline = min(deadline - reserve - 120.0, t0 + 500.0)
    if main_deadline < t0 + 20.0:
       main_deadline = min(deadline - reserve - 30.0, t0 + 100.0)
    h1,c1,_ = wcd_optimize(best_h, n_base, main_deadline, FINE_SLACKS)
    if c1 < best_c:
       best_h,best_c = project_feasible(h1,n_base), c1
    # Alternates
    wcd_stop = deadline - reserve - 60.0
    plans = []
    n1 = min(900, n_base + 200)
    if n1 != n_base: plans.append((n1,0,0.0))
    n2 = max(200, n_base // 2)
    if n2 != n_base: plans.append((n2,0,0.0))
    plans.append((n_base,1,0.02))
    plans.append((n_base,1,0.08))
    plans.append((n_base,2,1.5))
    plans.append((n_base,2,4.0))
    plans.append((n_base,2,9.0))
    for plan in plans:
       if time.time() > wcd_stop - 40: break
       n,kind,param=plan
       hstart=make_start(best_h,n,kind,param,rng)
       slacks = BROAD_SLACKS if kind != 1 else FINE_SLACKS
       # if ref kind 0 and n==n_base maybe skip? main already. We'll only add if different n.
       alt_deadline = min(wcd_stop, time.time() + 150.0)
       hopt,copt,_ = wcd_optimize(hstart,n,alt_deadline,slacks)
       if copt < best_c:
          best_c=copt; best_h=project_feasible(hopt,n)
          reserve=est_res(len(best_h))
          wcd_stop = min(wcd_stop, deadline - reserve - 60.0)
    # final exact
    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        best_h,best_c = exact_polish(best_h, len(best_h), deadline, rng)
    # final ensure feasible
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)

def enforce_sum(h,n):
 target=0.5*n

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.array(h,copy=True); h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=0.5*n
   for i in range(60):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         mask=h<1.0-1e-12
         if not mask.any(): break
         vals=h[mask]; room=(1-vals).sum()
         if room<=0: break
         h[mask]=np.clip(vals+diff/room,0,1)
      else:
         mask=h>1e-12
         if not mask.any(): break
         vals=h[mask]; tot=vals.sum();
         if tot<=0: break
         h[mask]=np.clip(vals+diff/tot,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       mask=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       if mask.any():
           idx=np.array(np.flatnonzero(mask))
           # choose one with enough room? distribute
           h[idx] += diff / len(idx)
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
```
This may not exact due to clip but diff tiny. Good.

import time

FINE_SLACKS = (0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.00008,0.00003)
BROAD_SLACKS = (0.03,0.015,0.007,0.003,0.0012,0.0005,0.0002,0.00008)

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

`project_feasible` as above.

`make_weights`:
Need `min_active` maybe 10. Use `min_active=10` for smaller n? For n=200 L=399, min10 okay. Top K 2.5%.
```
def make_weights(c,n,slack_count,min_active=10):
    L=c.size
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    level=cmax-slack_count
    x=np.maximum(c-level,0.0)
    act=int(np.count_nonzero(x>0.0))
    k=max(2, min(L, min_active))
    if act < k:
        if k < L:
            thr=float(np.partition(c, L-k)[L-k])
        else:
            thr=float(np.min(c))
        level2 = thr - 0.25
        if level2 < level: level2 = level
        x=np.maximum(c-level2,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(x))
    if s < 1e-14:
        x=np.zeros(L)
        if k < L:
            idx=np.argpartition(c, L-k)[L-k:]
        else: idx=np.arange(L)
        x[idx]=1.0
        s=float(x[idx].size)
    p2=x*x
    s2=float(np.sum(p2))
    if s2 > 1e-14:
       w=p2/s2
    else:
       w=x/s
    w += 1e-9
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
If x values large, p2 may overflow? c up to n maybe 2000, slack maybe counts <100, p2 <10000, fine.

`weighted_gradient` direct:
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w,n):
    N=n-1
    g1=np.convolve(1.0-h, w[::-1], mode='full')[N:N+n]
    g2=np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')[N:N+n]
    return g1-g2
```
Check `g1` length exactly? For n=700, conv len 4198? 3n-2=2098? Wait n=700,L=1399, n+L-1=2098 (not 4198). Slice N:N+n = 699:1399 length700. Conv len 2098, slice within. Good.

`wcd_optimize`:
```
def wcd_optimize(h,n,deadline,slacks,max_step=0.05,pass_cap=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    if pass_cap is None:
       if n <=800: pass_cap=250
       elif n<=1100: pass_cap=160
       else: pass_cap=100
    mu=0.2
    target=0.5*n
    for slC in slacks:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       c5=float(np.max(c))*(2.0/n)
       if c5 < best_c:
          best_c=c5; best_h=h.copy()
       if c5 > best_c + 0.02:
          h=best_h.copy()
       slack_count=max(0.02, 0.5*n*slC)
       w=make_weights(c,n,slack_count,10)
       G=weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
       pair=w+w[::-1]
       wN=float(w[n-1])
       Acoef=mu-wN
       passes=0; maxd=1.0
       while passes < pass_cap and maxd > 1e-9 and time.time() < deadline:
          passes += 1
          maxd=0.0
          diff=0.0
          for p in range(n):
             h0=float(h[p])
             lo=-h0; hi=1.0-h0
             if lo < -max_step: lo=-max_step
             if hi > max_step: hi=max_step
             if lo >= hi: continue
             B=G[p]+2.0*mu*diff
             if Acoef > 1e-8:
                d=-B/(2.0*Acoef)
                if d < lo: d=lo
                elif d > hi: d=hi
             else:
                v0=B*lo + Acoef*lo*lo
                v1=B*hi + Acoef*hi*hi
                if v1 < v0 - 1e-13:
                   d=hi
                elif v0 < v1 - 1e-13:
                   d=lo
                else:
                   if abs(h0-0.5) > 1e-6:
                      d=hi if h0 < 0.5 else lo
                   else:
                      d=0.5 if (p & 1) == 0 else -0.5
                      if d < lo: d=lo
                      elif d > hi: d=hi
             if abs(d) > 1e-12:
                h[p]=h0+d
                diff += d
                if abs(d) > maxd: maxd=abs(d)
                N=n-1
                qlo=p-N
                if qlo < 0: qlo=0
                qhi=p+N
                if qhi >= n: qhi=n-1
                klo=N+(qlo-p)
                lnq=qhi-qlo+1
                G[qlo:qhi+1] -= pair[klo:klo+lnq]*d
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          G=weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
       c5=eval_c5(h,n)
       if c5 < best_c:
          best_c=c5; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c,h
```

If `lo>=hi` skip due to max_step 0; max_step positive.
`pair` update: pair length L, klo:N? For qlo=0 when p<N, klo=N-p >=0; qhi=n-1 maybe, klo+lnq =? qhi=p+N? In middle, klo+lnq = N+(qhi-p) ≤ 2N=L-1. Good.
Projection every pass; G reinit. `diff` reset; no need after project.
`if c5 > best_c + 0.02: h=best_h.copy()` before weights; for broad random, local best may be high, current lower? If current higher by 0.02, reset. In early broad, current could worsen by >0.02 while still on path; resetting may halt. Threshold 0.02 large; okay.
Maybe for random broad, initial c 0.5, after first stage c 0.45 lower, best updated. If after second c 0.48 > best+0.02? 0.48>0.47, reset to 0.45; okay. Could still need worsening >0.02 to escape? Rare. Use 0.05 to allow. Since local best is for this start, 0.05. Use `0.03`.

`exact_polish`:
Need pass `deadline`. Use `step = 0.05` maybe `D=0.15`? If step 0.05, diff maximum 0.05 per update, but allowed by D. Set D=0.1. If a coordinate wants 0.05, okay. Use `max_passes=6` or time. ```
def exact_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    current_cnt=float(np.max(c))
    dx=2.0/n
    D=0.1; step=0.05
    passes=0
    while passes<8 and time.time() < deadline:
       passes += 1
       max_move=0.0
       diff=0.0
       if passes == 1:
          order=list(range(n))
       else:
          order=list(rng.permutation(n))
       for p in order:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          h0=float(h[p])
          lo=-h0
          if lo > -D - diff? Actually lower allowed by sum: -D - diff. Need dlo ≥ -D - diff? condition |diff+d|≤D => -D ≤ diff+d ≤ D => d ≥ -D-diff and d ≤ D-diff.
          dlo = max(-h0, -D - diff, -step)
          dhi = min(1-h0, D - diff, step)
          if dlo > dhi: continue
          if dlo == 0 and dhi == 0: continue
          a=coord_a(h,p,n)
          d=propose_d(c,a,h0,dlo,dhi,n)
          if abs(d) > 1e-11:
             c += a*d
             c[n-1] -= d*d
             new_cnt=float(np.max(c))
             if new_cnt <= current_cnt + 1e-8:
                h[p]=h0+d
                diff += d
                current_cnt = new_cnt if new_cnt < current_cnt else current_cnt
                if abs(d)>max_move: max_move=abs(d)
             else:
                c -= a*d
                c[n-1] += d*d
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          diff=0.0
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       current_cnt=float(np.max(c))
       c5=current_cnt*dx
       if c5 < best_c:
          best_c=c5; best_h=h.copy()
       if max_move < 1e-8:
          break
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need `order=list` could be memory for n=1000, fine. `rng.permutation` returns array; iter. If no rng? pass.
Potential issue: `c` modified in place; in propose_d, `temp=c+a*d` creates new; after accepted we do `c += a*d`. But `a` no longer needed. Good.
If rejected, `c -= a*d` restores exactly? Since `c +=` then `-=`, floating round maybe tiny. Could recompute? Rare. Or use `c = c.copy()`? no.
If `new_cnt` from c.max after commit, then we update c; okay.
Since c incremental maybe drift; recompute each pass.

`propose_d` uses `np.any(zero)`; zero mask boolean length L. Good.

Now `make_start`:
```
def make_start(ref_h,n,kind,param,rng):
    if len(ref_h) == n and kind != 2:
        base=ref_h.copy()
    else:
        xold=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(ref_h),endpoint=True)
        xnew=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=True)
        base=np.interp(xnew,xold,ref_h)
    if kind == 1:
        base = base + rng.normal(0.0,float(param),n)
    elif kind == 2:
        x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
        f=float(param)
        ph1=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
        ph2=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
        base = 0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(math.pi*f*x + ph1) + 0.18*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*f*x + ph2)
        # optionally reflect? not
    return project_feasible(base,n)
```
`math.pi*f*x` scalar times array okay.
For kind 0, if ref_h length n, copy; else interpolate. For kind 1, base noise. Project.

def est_reserve(n):
    if n <= 700: return 180
    if n <= 900: return 220
    return 260

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
   t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3.0
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   try:
      base_h=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
      n_base=int(base_h.size)
      if n_base < 50: n_base=50; base_h=np.linspace(0,1,n_base)
   except Exception:
      n_base=500; base_h=np.full(n_base,0.5)
   base_h=project_feasible(base_h,n_base)
   if float(np.std(base_h)) < 1e-7:
      base_h=make_start(base_h,n_base,2,3.0,rng)
   best_h=base_h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n_base)
   reserve=est_reserve(n_base)
   main_deadline=min(deadline-reserve-150.0, t0+450.0)
   if main_deadline < t0+30.0:
       main_deadline=min(deadline-reserve-30.0, t0+120.0)
   h1,c1,_=wcd_optimize(best_h,n_base,main_deadline,FINE_SLACKS,0.05)
   if c1 < best_c:
      best_c=c1; best_h=project_feasible(h1,n_base)
   wcd_stop=deadline-reserve-80.0
   plans=[]
   nA=min(900,n_base+200)
   if nA != n_base: plans.append((nA,0,0.0))
   nB=max(200,n_base//2)
   if nB != n_base: plans.append((nB,0,0.0))
   plans.append((n_base,1,0.015))
   plans.append((n_base,1,0.06))
   plans.append((n_base,2,1.5))
   plans.append((n_base,2,4.0))
   plans.append((n_base,2,9.0))
   for plan in plans:
      if time.time() > max(t0+1.0, wcd_stop)-60.0: break
      n,kind,param=plan
      # If kind 0 and n == len(best_h)? We may have already main, skip if just done? To avoid duplicate, if kind==0 and n==len(best_h) and plan is first? Simpler skip plan (n_base,0,0) not added.
      hstart=make_start(best_h,n,kind,param,rng)
      sl = BROAD_SLACKS if kind in (0,2) else FINE_SLACKS
      maxst = 0.3 if kind in (0,2) else 0.05
      alt_deadline = min(wcd_stop, time.time() + 160.0)
      hopt,copt,_ = wcd_optimize(hstart,n,alt_deadline,sl,maxst)
      if copt < best_c:
          best_c=copt; best_h=project_feasible(hopt,n)
          reserve=est_reserve(n)
          wcd_stop=min(wcd_stop, deadline-reserve-80.0)
   if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
       best_h,best_c=exact_polish(best_h,len(best_h),deadline,rng)
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
   best_c=eval_c5(best_h,len(best_h))
   return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)
```

- If p is second in (1-h_i)_i=p: derivative -h_{p+q}. Negative (1-h[jf])? Actually -h[p+q].
So derivative coefficients: positive for backward jb=p-m+N, negative for forward jf=p+m-N. Previous code had positive for forward (jf) and negative for backward (jb), i.e., opposite! Did previous code have bug? Let's re-read previous solve_1d_bisection:
```
jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
jb = p - ms + (n - 1)

valid_b...

```

It will oscillate p even 0 then project, maxd never small, pass_cap stops. Best unchanged. For n=1 not used. For flat n large, projection may keep sum but moves.

Tie-breaking with p parity and then projection could cause unbalanced sum; projection proportional. It may create checkerboard but sum exact. Good.

One concern: In WCD, for A<0 and endpoints equal, choosing p parity can cause all moves one direction? p even move +step, odd -step; over a pass h becomes alternating up/down by step. Projection to mean may leave pattern. Good.

Now, possible issue with `project_feasible` distributing to all non-bound proportional can turn checkerboard 0/1 back to 0.5 if sum off? If checkerboard sum exactly target (n even) no change. If sum off, proportion to room/mass may set many near fractional; okay.

Now, if initial_h_values is available, main WCD FINE from good h: At first stage, wN maybe active? Suppose active lags top central? If wN small, A positive, smooth. If wN large, A negative, endpoint moves within max_step; may jump. Could degrade; local reset if >best+0.03. But if initial best 0.38086, any >0.3838 reset. Good.

Now, if WCD main worstens local best due to `[0.03]` reset, it may stay at initial and no improvement. Alternates perturb/noisy with broad may find lower. Good.

Could exact_polish from initial (if WCD didn't improve) find last tiny improvement? It starts at best_h. It may reduce by 1e-5. Good.

Now, ensure `run` returns tuple with `n_points` int. `len(best_h)` returns int.

Let's write final code. Need avoid too many comments but okay. Ensure no lambda. Use `np.clip` etc.

One more check: In `exact_polish`, we create `a=coord_a(h,p,n)` for every p. If `dlo==dhi` skip. If dval chosen 0, no move. `propose_d` can be expensive; if h0 at bound and interval has only one direction small, okay. We can skip if `dlo < 0`? no.

Potential bug: `propose_d` bisection uses `czero = float(np.max(c[zero]))` where zero mask includes positions with |a|≤1e-13. But for positions where a is zero and c is largest, L must be >= czero. Since d=0 feasible, czero≤cmax. Good. If no zero positions, set -1e30. In feasible, if czero > L, infeas. With czero -1e30, fine.

In feasible, divisions by apos/aneg could produce inf if L-cpos negative and apos positive? finite. If `apos` values very small? Mask eps=1e-13, c ~1e3, ratio ~1e16 finite. hi might become huge negative; lo/hi compare. Fine.
Could set `np.errstate`? Not needed.

In `propose_d`, candidates list includes roots maybe outside [dlo,dhi]; we clip before evaluate? Code:
```
for d in cands:
 if d < dlo - 1e-12 or d > dhi + 1e-12: continue
 d = float(np.clip(d,dlo,dhi))
 ...
```
If root just outside, skip; okay. If candidate within but due to clip, good.
If best_res None (shouldn't), cands just interval endpoints and 0. Best maybe 0.

In exact_polish, after pass recompute c with `np.correlate`; if best_h from local best before projection? We project best at end. Good.

Now, if np.correlate returns small negative due to floating? c from products nonneg; exact. czero -1e30. Good.

Let's now produce final code. Ensure fence. No analysis in final. Need only code. final with code block.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

FINE_SLACKS = (0.004, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.00008, 0.00003)
BROAD_SLACKS = (0.03, 0.015, 0.007, 0.003, 0.0012, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.00008)


def eval_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(60):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            vals = h[mask]
            room = float(np.sum(1.0 - vals))
            if room <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] = np.clip(vals + diff / room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            vals = h[mask]
            tot = float(np.sum(vals))
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] = np.clip(vals + diff / tot, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        mask = (h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if np.any(mask):
            idx = np.flatnonzero(mask)
            h[idx] = np.clip(h[idx] + diff / len(idx), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def make_weights(c, n, slack_count, min_active=10):
    L = int(c.size)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    level = cmax - slack_count
    x = np.maximum(c - level, 0.0)
    active = int(np.count_nonzero(x > 0.0))
    k = max(2, min(L, min_active))
    if active < k:
        if k < L:
            thr = float(np.partition(c, L - k)[L - k])
        else:
            thr = float(np.min(c))
        level2 = thr - 0.25
        if level2 < level:
            level2 = level
        x = np.maximum(c - level2, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s < 1e-14:
        x = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        if k < L:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L - k)[L - k:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        x[idx] = 1.0
        s = float(x[idx].size)
    p2 = x * x
    s2 = float(np.sum(p2))
    if s2 > 1e-14:
        w = p2 / s2
    else:
        w = x / s
    w += 1e-9
    w /= float(np.sum(w))
    return w


def weighted_gradient(h, w, n):
    N = n - 1
    g1 = np.convolve(1.0 - h, w[::-1], mode="full")[N:N + n]
    g2 = np.convolve(h, w, mode="full")[N:N + n]
    return g1 - g2


def wcd_optimize(h, n, deadline, slacks, max_step=0.05, pass_cap=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h, n)
    if pass_cap is None:
        if n <= 800:
            pass_cap = 260
        elif n <= 1100:
            pass_cap = 170
        else:
            pass_cap = 110
    mu = 0.2
    for slC in slacks:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        c5 = float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()
        if c5 > best_c + 0.03:
            h = best_h.copy()
        slack_count = max(0.02, 0.5 * n * slC)
        w = make_weights(c, n, slack_count, 10)
        G = weighted_gradient(h, w, n)
        pair = w + w[::-1]
        wN = float(w[n - 1])
        Acoef = mu - wN
        passes = 0
        maxd = 1.0
        while passes < pass_cap and maxd > 1e-9 and time.time() < deadline:
            passes += 1
            maxd = 0.0
            diff = 0.0
            for p in range(n):
                h0 = float(h[p])
                lo = -h0
                hi = 1.0 - h0
                if lo < -max_step:
                    lo = -max_step
                if hi > max_step:
                    hi = max_step
                if lo >= hi:
                    continue
                B = G[p] + 2.0 * mu * diff
                if Acoef > 1e-8:
                    d = -B / (2.0 * Acoef)
                    if d < lo:
                        d = lo
                    elif d > hi:
                        d = hi
                else:
                    v0 = B * lo + Acoef * lo * lo
                    v1 = B * hi + Acoef * hi * hi
                    if v1 < v0 - 1e-13:
                        d = hi
                    elif v0 < v1 - 1e-13:
                        d = lo
                    else:
                        if abs(h0 - 0.5) > 1e-6:
                            d = hi if h0 < 0.5 else lo
                        else:
                            d = 0.5 if (p & 1) == 0 else -0.5
                            if d < lo:
                                d = lo
                            elif d > hi:
                                d = hi
                if abs(d) > 1e-12:
                    h[p] = h0 + d
                    diff += d
                    if abs(d) > maxd:
                        maxd = abs(d)
                    N = n - 1
                    qlo = p - N
                    if qlo < 0:
                        qlo = 0
                    qhi = p + N
                    if qhi >= n:
                        qhi = n - 1
                    klo = N + (qlo - p)
                    lnq = qhi - qlo + 1
                    G[qlo:qhi + 1] -= pair[klo:klo + lnq] * d
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            G = weighted_gradient(h, w, n)
        c5 = eval_c5(h, n)
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c, h


def reverse_slice(arr, start, length):
    if length <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    stop = start - length
    if stop >= 0:
        return arr[start:stop:-1]
    return arr[start::-1][:length]


def coord_a(h, p, n):
    N = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    m1 = N - p
    if m1 < 0:
        m1 = 0
    m2 = 2 * N - p
    if m2 > L - 1:
        m2 = L - 1
    if m1 <= m2:
        ln = m2 - m1 + 1
        jf = p + m1 - N
        a[m1:m2 + 1] += 1.0 - h[jf:jf + ln]
    m1b = p
    if m1b < 0:
        m1b = 0
    m2b = p + N
    if m2b > L - 1:
        m2b = L - 1
    if m1b <= m2b:
        ln = m2b - m1b + 1
        jb = p - m1b + N
        a[m1b:m2b + 1] -= reverse_slice(h, jb, ln)
    return a


def exact_feasible(czero, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, diag_c, diag_a, Lval, dlo, dhi):
    lo = dlo
    hi = dhi
    if czero > Lval + 1e-12:
        return None
    if apos.size > 0:
        v = float(np.min((Lval - cpos) / apos))
        if v < hi:
            hi = v
    if aneg.size > 0:
        v = float(np.max((Lval - cneg) / aneg))
        if v > lo:
            lo = v
    if lo > hi + 1e-11:
        return None
    B = diag_a
    C = Lval - diag_c
    disc = B * B - 4.0 * C
    roots = None
    if disc >= 0.0:
        rt = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
        r1 = 0.5 * (B - rt)
        r2 = 0.5 * (B + rt)
        roots = (r1, r2)
        if lo > r1 + 1e-11 and hi < r2 - 1e-11:
            return None
    if lo < dlo:
        lo = dlo
    if hi > dhi:
        hi = dhi
    if lo > hi + 1e-11:
        return None
    return lo, hi, roots


def propose_d(c, a, h0, dlo, dhi, n):
    if dlo > dhi:
        return 0.0
    N = n - 1
    eps = 1e-13
    pos = a > eps
    neg = a < -eps
    cpos = c[pos]
    apos = a[pos]
    cneg = c[neg]
    aneg = a[neg]
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    czero = float(np.max(c[zero])) if np.any(zero) else -1e30
    diag_c = float(c[N])
    diag_a = float(a[N])
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    loL = 0.0
    hiL = cmax
    best_res = None
    for _ in range(26):
        Lval = 0.5 * (loL + hiL)
        res = exact_feasible(czero, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, diag_c, diag_a, Lval, dlo, dhi)
        if res is None:
            loL = Lval
        else:
            hiL = Lval
            best_res = res
    cands = [dlo, dhi, 0.0]
    if best_res is not None:
        lo, hi, roots = best_res
        cands.extend([lo, hi, 0.5 * (lo + hi)])
        if roots is not None:
            cands.extend(list(roots))
    best_d = 0.0
    best_val = cmax
    for d in cands:
        if d < dlo - 1e-12 or d > dhi + 1e-12:
            continue
        d = float(min(max(d, dlo), dhi))
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            val = cmax
        else:
            tmp = c + a * d
            tmp[N] -= d * d
            val = float(np.max(tmp))
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_d = d
    return best_d


def exact_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    current_cnt = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0 / n
    D = 0.15
    step = 0.08
    passes = 0
    while passes < 8 and time.time() < deadline:
        passes += 1
        max_move = 0.0
        diff = 0.0
        if passes == 1:
            order = range(n)
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            dlo = max(-h0, -D - diff, -step)
            dhi = min(1.0 - h0, D - diff, step)
            if dlo > dhi:
                continue
            a = coord_a(h, p, n)
            d = propose_d(c, a, h0, dlo, dhi, n)
            if abs(d) > 1e-11:
                new_c = c + a * d
                new_c[n - 1] -= d * d
                new_cnt = float(np.max(new_c))
                if new_cnt <= current_cnt + 1e-8:
                    c = new_c
                    h[p] = h0 + d
                    diff += d
                    if new_cnt < current_cnt:
                        current_cnt = new_cnt
                    if abs(d) > max_move:
                        max_move = abs(d)
        if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            diff = 0.0
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        current_cnt = float(np.max(c))
        c5 = current_cnt * dx
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()
        if max_move < 1e-8:
            break
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def make_start(ref_h, n, kind, param, rng):
    if len(ref_h) == n and kind != 2:
        base = ref_h.copy()
    else:
        xold = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(ref_h), endpoint=True)
        xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=True)
        base = np.interp(xnew, xold, ref_h)
    if kind == 1:
        base = base + rng.normal(0.0, float(param), n)
    elif kind == 2:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=True)
        f = float(param)
        ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        base = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(math.pi * f * x + ph1) + 0.18 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph2)
    return project_feasible(base, n)


def est_reserve(n):
    if n <= 700:
        return 180
    if n <= 900:
        return 220
    return 260


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        base_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n_base = int(base_h.size)
        if n_base < 50:
            n_base = 50
            base_h = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_base)
    except Exception:
        n_base = 500
        base_h = np.full(n_base, 0.5)

    base_h = project_feasible(base_h, n_base)
    if float(np.std(base_h)) < 1e-7:
        base_h = make_start(base_h, n_base, 2, 3.0, rng)

    best_h = base_h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, n_base)
    reserve = est_reserve(n_base)

    main_deadline = min(deadline - reserve - 150.0, t0 + 450.0)
    if main_deadline < t0 + 30.0:
        main_deadline = min(deadline - reserve - 30.0, t0 + 120.0)

    h1, c1, _ = wcd_optimize(best_h, n_base, main_deadline, FINE_SLACKS, 0.05)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_c = c1
        best_h = project_feasible(h1, n_base)

    wcd_stop = deadline - reserve - 80.0
    plans = []
    nA = min(900, n_base + 200)
    if nA != n_base:
        plans.append((nA, 0, 0.0))
    nB = max(200, n_base // 2)
    if nB != n_base:
        plans.append((nB, 0, 0.0))
    plans.append((n_base, 1, 0.015))
    plans.append((n_base, 1, 0.06))
    plans.append((n_base, 2, 1.5))
    plans.append((n_base, 2, 4.0))
    plans.append((n_base, 2, 9.0))

    for plan in plans:
        if time.time() > max(t0 + 1.0, wcd_stop) - 60.0:
            break
        n, kind, param = plan
        hstart = make_start(best_h, n, kind, param, rng)
        if kind == 0:
            slacks = BROAD_SLACKS
            maxst = 0.2
        elif kind == 2:
            slacks = BROAD_SLACKS
            maxst = 0.3
        else:
            slacks = FINE_SLACKS
            maxst = 0.05
        alt_deadline = min(wcd_stop, time.time() + 160.0)
        hopt, copt, _ = wcd_optimize(hstart, n, alt_deadline, slacks, maxst)
        if copt < best_c:
            best_c = copt
            best_h = project_feasible(hopt, n)
            reserve = est_reserve(n)
            wcd_stop = min(wcd_stop, deadline - reserve - 80.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
        best_h, best_c = exact_polish(best_h, len(best_h), deadline, rng)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
